# All escort ship modifications

# Corvette - Armor (T1 --> T5)
ship_mod_corvette_armor_tier_1 = {	# Postwar Structural Protection
	type = ship_mod_slot_armor

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/military_icons/navy_icons/ship_designer_icons/ship_designer_armor_low.dds"
	clicksound = "event:/SFX/UI/ShipDesigner/shipdesigner_modification_armor_light"

	modifier = {
		ship_armor_add = 10
		ship_hit_points_max_add = 150
		ship_suffered_crit_chance_mult = -0.02
		ship_suffered_hull_damage_mult = -0.03
		ship_vulnerability_add = -3
	}

	construction_goods = {
		goods_input_iron_add = 23
		goods_input_steel_add = 75
	}

	materiel_goods = {
		goods_input_steel_add = 0.019
	}

	ai_weight = {
		value = 1
		multiply = ship_mod_market_multiplier
	}
}

		ship_crew_experience_gain_add = 0.02

		ship_battle_against_ship_type_guided_missile_corvette_accuracy_add = 0.007
		ship_battle_against_ship_type_guided_missile_corvette_accuracy_mult = 0.011

		ship_battle_against_ship_type_guided_missile_frigate_accuracy_add = 0.005
		ship_battle_against_ship_type_guided_missile_frigate_accuracy_mult = 0.007

		ship_battle_against_ship_type_modern_corvette_accuracy_add = 0.007
		ship_battle_against_ship_type_modern_corvette_accuracy_mult = 0.011

		ship_battle_against_ship_type_modern_frigate_accuracy_add = 0.005
